# Kennel Onboarding — Target Queue

Ranked backlog for the daily onboarding task. **Rank 1 = onboard next.** The daily run
([`daily-onboarding-prompt.md`](daily-onboarding-prompt.md)) takes the top `queued` row each
morning. When `queued` rows drop to **5 or fewer**, the run refills the list back to ~20.

**Inclusion rule:** every entry must have a **confirmed/identified dynamic data source**
(Google Calendar, iCal, Meetup, Harrier Central, Hash Rego, Google Sheets, or a scrapeable website)
that is **live and recently active** — upcoming events OR a regular cadence whose latest run is
within ~2× its interval (the *recently-active* rule; 0 upcoming alone is fine). No Facebook-only /
static-only kennels.

**Dedup rule (see prompt Step 2) — against the LIVE sitemap, read via the Chrome MCP.** The seed
files are incomplete (live site has 410+ kennel slugs in the sitemap vs ~349 in seed; kennels are
added straight to prod via the admin UI), the prod DB isn't reachable from the run's sandbox, and
the kennel directory is client-rendered. The authoritative oracle is `hashtracks.xyz/sitemap.xml`
read through the Chrome MCP. **Slugs aren't always the obvious kebab** — scan for fragments
(Singapore Harriets = `sg-harriets`, Miami Valley = `mvh3-day`, HK Monday = `hk-h3`, Brussels
Manneke Piss = `bmph3`).

**Status values:** `queued` · `in_progress` · `handed-off` · `blocked` · `done (already live)` · `source-add` · `verify-live-first` · `shipped`

> Cooperative: John can reorder, add, or strike rows anytime. The daily run respects the current
> top `queued` row.

## How this doc is organized

- **[Active queue](#active-queue-queued--onboard-next) (`queued`)** — ready to onboard now; the daily run takes the top row.
- **[Needs source verification](#needs-source-verification-verify-live-first) (`verify-live-first`)** — promising kennels whose live dynamic source must be confirmed (often via browserRender/Chrome) before they can be queued.
- **[Handed off — awaiting implementation](#handed-off--awaiting-implementation)** — research + handoff doc complete; awaiting Claude Code build/PR.
- **[Shipped / live in prod](#shipped--live-in-prod)** — fully onboarded (`shipped` or `done (already live)`); reference only.
- **[Blocked / dormant](#blocked--dormant)** — verified no live dynamic source; re-check on refill.
- **[Leads](#leads--verify-a-live-dynamic-source-before-queueing) · [Removed (already live)](#removed--already-live-authoritative-sitemap-check) · [Parking lot](#parking-lot--rejected-no-usable-dynamic-source)** — candidate bench, dedup exclusions, and rejects.
- **[Sitemap audit history](#sitemap-audit-history-chronological)** — chronological dedup/audit log (moved to the bottom).

### Source mechanics confirmed (2026-06-18 Claude-in-Chrome recon) — read alongside the rows above

Feed/SSR/archive confirmed for the 6 website kennels so each handoff is build-ready (no "is it config-only?" guesswork):

- **Riyadh H3 ⭐ — clean JSON API (NOT a scrape).** Supabase PostgREST: `GET https://uleyjftvdnpniabomdpi.supabase.co/rest/v1/hikes?select=*&order=date.desc` with the project's **public `anon` JWT** (role `anon`, decoded from the JS bundle `index-DoGZHVwU.js` — a publishable client key, safe to embed like a Maps key; ⚠️ Claude Code: re-extract it at build, it can rotate). Returns rows like `{"run_number":"2492","title":"Near Falcon Valley","date":"2026-06-19","location":"Ammairyah","difficulty":"moderate","gathering_time":"16:30:00","circle_time":"17:30:00","registration_status":"active","deleted_at":null}`. **CSR (no SSR)** → must use the API. **Full backfill in one query** — 58 rows back to 2025-01-03 (#2415); filter `deleted_at=is.null`. NEW lightweight JSON adapter (not HTML). `startTime` from `gathering_time` (16:30). Weekly Thursday.
- **Desert H3 — static Cheerio (wp-json DISABLED).** Whole WP REST API 404s on the LiteSpeed host. Home page embeds **JSON-LD `@type:Event`** blocks in raw HTML (SSR) — parse those. **Archive: the Hare Line page `/?page_id=5152`** lists the last ~50 runs (SSR, back to #2405 / June 2025) with a LOAD MORE (`admin-ajax.php`) for older → one-shot backfill possible. Calendar = Modern Events Calendar (no public collection `.ics`). Mon 19:00; fees 50 AED villa / 10 AED pub; FB `facebook.com/BarbieHasher`; logo `deserthash.org/wp-content/uploads/2018/11/DH3MastHead2.png`.
- **Creek H3 — static Cheerio (no JSON).** Home SSRs "This Week's Meet Point" (`{date} – Run {#} – {venue}` → post link). **Archive: WP category pages — `/?cat=4`** ("Creek Runs", clean SSR paginated list of `{date} – Run {#} – {venue}` titles; `/?cat=14` = Xmas Runs) → backfill. Men-only Thu; logo `creekhash.org/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/NewCreekHeaderv3.png`.
- **Bombay H3 — WP REST page content (no event post type).** Runs live in the **Home page**: `GET /wp-json/wp/v2/pages/930?_fields=content,modified` (parse the `RUN #NNN … {date}` HTML blocks; SSR mirror on the home page). **No real archive** — site created Feb 2026, history only back to **#627 (22 Feb 2026)**. Founded **6 Feb 1983**; ~monthly Sun 09:30; ₹250 early/₹400 late; logo `bombayhash.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/cropped-…-165x105.png`. ⚠️ embedded waiver form on the page — don't submit.
- **Colombo Harriettes — static Cheerio, CONFIRMED SSR (no feed).** Next.js, the "Next run" block is in the **raw server HTML** (no API/feed; only a Google Maps embed). Single current run, **no archive**. Sat 17:00.
- **Colombo H3 men — own JSON API (for when it refreshes).** Next.js route handlers: `GET /api/locations` → single current run-site `{name,lat,lng,date}`; `/api/events` → specials (`[]`). Also SSR on home. No archive/history param. Currently stale (24 May).
- 🔑 **`hashruns.org/api/global-runs` serves PAST events too** (not just discovery) → it's a **one-shot backfill source for ANY Harrier Central kennel** (Bandung 56 rows, Moonshine 17, Barbados 130 all pulled with dates/run#/hares/lat-lng/fees). Caveats from the pull: future window caps at ~200 rows; pull past in ≤6-month windows (≤2 MB cap); some kennels have source data quirks (Bandung dup run# 2292, `Hares` field sometimes carries the location — treat HC `Hares` as unreliable for Bandung).

## Leads — verify a live dynamic source before queueing

Grep-confirmed absent from the live sitemap but need one more live check before promotion. **Promotion
bar = a live dynamic source with recent-cadence activity — NOT necessarily an upcoming run.** Apply
the *recently-active* rule (daily-prompt Step 3): a regular-cadence kennel whose latest run is within
~2× its interval qualifies even at 0 upcoming (onboard with a recent-history backfill).

### 2026-06-18 discovery + Harrier Central sweep (Claude-in-Chrome) — 5 promotions + config-only goldmine

A wide uncovered-country hunt + a Harrier Central sweep via the in-browser extension. **Promoted 5 to the Active queue** (ranks 6–10, all sitemap-deduped at 446): 🇦🇪 **Desert H3** (Dubai, deserthash.org — 1st UAE), 🇮🇩 **Bandung H3** (config-only HC), 🇸🇦 **Riyadh H3** (1st Saudi), 🇮🇳 **Bombay H3** (1st India), 🇦🇪 **Creek H3** (2nd UAE).

🔑 **Reusable Harrier Central discovery method (better than the Azure token sweep):** `harriercentral.com` has **no public web directory** (the DB is app-only; `/index.php/kennels/` is empty). But **`hashruns.org` is the public web front-end for the same data** ("Powered by Harrier Central") with a clean read-only JSON endpoint:
`https://hashruns.org/api/global-runs?isFuture={0|1}&minEventDate=YYYY-MM-DD&maxEventDate=YYYY-MM-DD`
— each record carries `PublicKennelId` (GUID), `KennelSlug`, `KennelShortName`, `KennelName`, `KennelContinent`, `KennelIANATimezone`, plus per-event number/name/datetime/hares/location/lat-lng/fees/tags. **This enumerates every HC kennel that has posted a run** (the right filter for us). A 12-month pull = 2,924 events → **128 distinct active kennels worldwide**, overwhelmingly in already-covered regions. (See `source-platform-notes.md` → Harrier Central for the recipe.)

**Lead-tier HC finds (config-only when promoted — GUIDs captured):**

### 2026-06-25 — Are the SA Facebook links usable via FACEBOOK_HOSTED_EVENTS? (mostly no)

The adapter only works on public **Pages** with a populated `/upcoming_hosted_events` tab — NOT Facebook **Groups** (login-gated, no public events tab). Classifying every SA FB link:
- **Groups (unusable):** Rio (`groups/riohash`), Brazil Nuts/São Paulo (`groups/bnhhh`), Porto Alegre (`groups/719140093739270`), Bogotá (`groups/285354801526384`), Medellín (`groups/medellinh3`), Quito (`groups/429110172115301`), Cuenca (`groups/332689577610014`), La Paz (`groups/lapazhash`), Lima (`groups/14448170326`) + Lima Extra (`groups/349135720432135`), Buenos Aires (`groups/19722699147`), Paramaribo (`groups/180329322468658`), Guyana (`groups/154412853478`), Asunción (`groups/1254316384994089`, already live via WP). → **all out.**
- **Pages — ✅ USER-VERIFIED 2026-06-25 (John): both Pages exist & are public.** **Uruguay H3** (`facebook.com/Uruguay-Hash-House-Harriers-156474934386144`) and **Santiago H3** (`facebook.com/shhhchile`). FACEBOOK_HOSTED_EVENTS adapter applies → would be a **config-only source-add** (adapter exists; the sandbox saw an empty body only because FB walls datacenter IPs, not because the Pages are gone). Brasília's Page (`facebook.com/BrasiliaHHH`) is already live via its Blogspot.
- 🟡 **BUT low yield → deprioritized.** John confirms they use the FB **Events** feature only **intermittently** — most run details go out as an **image post to the public page** (which the hosted-events tab does NOT capture) and activity has **shifted toward Instagram** (no public events API → not a source). So a FB-Events-only source returns 0 most weeks.
- **Recommended onboard shape when eventually picked (do NOT ship FB-only):** pair a **STATIC_SCHEDULE backbone** (Uruguay = biweekly; Santiago = monthly 3rd Saturday — keeps the kennel page populated every cycle) with **FACEBOOK_HOSTED_EVENTS** as enrichment for the occasional real FB Event. Mirror the **Grand Strand H3** precedent (STATIC_SCHEDULE low-trust fallback + FB primary). The FB leg must route through the NAS residential proxy (`config.useResidentialProxy:true`). Uruguay = first 🇺🇾, Santiago = first 🇨🇱 (revives the parked target).
- **Priority:** source **verified, low-priority** — kept in `verify-live-first` (NOT promoted into the Active `queued` table) so the daily run won't pick them ahead of higher-yield targets. Promote if either kennel resumes consistent FB Events, or onboard opportunistically as a STATIC_SCHEDULE+FB pair when the backlog is thin.
- **Santiago H3 (Chile)** — Blogspot `santiagohashhouseharriers.blogspot.com` last *run post* is **#176, June 2015** (121 posts; feed `updated 2026-05-06` is a page edit, not a new run); Meetup `santiago-hash-house-harriers` shows **0 upcoming** (Apollo `apolloEventCount:0`, 2026-06-03). *(2026-06-16: `shhh.cl` redirects to the same Meetup — still 0 upcoming, newest visible event Dec 2025.)* 🔁 **2026-06-25 — partially unparked:** its **FB Page `facebook.com/shhhchile` is user-verified public** (John) → a FACEBOOK_HOSTED_EVENTS path exists, but FB Events are used only **intermittently** (image posts / Instagram shift). Moved to **`verify-live-first`, low priority**: onboard as **STATIC_SCHEDULE (monthly 3rd Sat) backbone + FACEBOOK_HOSTED_EVENTS enrichment** (Grand Strand pattern; FB via `useResidentialProxy:true`) when the backlog is thin — would be **first 🇨🇱**. See the "SA Facebook links" note in the leads section.
- **Buenos Aires H3 (Argentina)** — Blogspot `buenosaireshashhouseharriers.blogspot.com` last run **#739, March 2020** (COVID-ended; 265 posts, feed last updated 2025-02). Stale ~6 yr. No live source.
